## Active summary & next action

**Pilot model retired 2026-05-27 (master D-046 / D-PROJ00-029; Project-03 record D-PROJ03-014).** Production is now open across all classes & subjects, gated **per class × subject × division** by the §5 D-005/D-042/D-045 gates. Class 1 Bangla below remains the **first production target** (most-prepared), not a cap; the §C rollout is no longer gated behind a Class 1 Bangla lock.

**Where we are:** Project 03 is stood up and the Class 1 Bangla annotated-skeleton field-test has run — it exposed a curation scan-miss (a theme/spot-check under-counted triggers: 0 → **14 forced + 4 borderline** on a full element rescan). That is now fixed upstream: **REF-01 → v1.2** (four §4.5 edge cases) + **REF-21** (the element-level scan protocol) + master **D-045** (the scan lock-gate). Gates 1–4 for Class 1 Bangla remain satisfied (2026-05-25); the annotated skeleton is now built under the REF-21 gate.

**Next action (in order):**
1. Apply the Project 03 Instructions REF-21 patches (§5 source-scan gate, §10 scan method, §16 checklist items, §17 Borderline register); ensure REF-21 + REF-01 v1.2 are available to the Project.
2. Cross-project follow-up still open: in **Project 02**, mark curation flags F-01/F-02/F-03/F-04 (and now F-05/F-06) ✅ Absorbed (D-PROJ03-011/013). *(Project 00 REF-05 index pointer is done — INDEX v1.22; D-PROJ03-012 closed.)*
3. ✅ **Done — Step A rebuilt & LOCKED (2026-05-26)** (chat `P03 · C1 BAN · Skeleton`). `LOCKED_C1_BAN_AnnotatedSkeleton_v1.md` produced under the REF-21 gate (54-পাঠ ledger + completeness assertion + 16 forced specs + 2 FLEXIBLE + Principal-ruled Borderline register → 6 REF-01 §4.5 edge-case proposals). পাঠ-count confirmed **54** (no Project-01 issue). **Next:** Project 05 authors RC-BAN-C1-U09 / U13 / U14 (pilot-scope flagged পাঠ) → then Step B lesson plans, shaped by period count (D-PROJ03-018). **2026-06-24 — U22 Session Plans in production:** ChapterPlan superseded v1→v2 (D-039, B5 দীঘি→হীরা purge); `LOCKED_C1_BAN_U22_S01_SessionPlan_v1` + `…_S02_…` both LOCKED (KEEP-AS-IS, mirrored to Chapter v2 Session-Map). Each carries one accepted [OPEN-RITUAL] soft-warn — 4th ritual = হাজিরা per REF-02 §3.1; Principal-authorized pending B-LP.1c.

**First target at a glance (D-046 — pilot model retired):** Class 1 Bangla পাঠ 1–16 is the most-prepared slice, **not a cap.** Workflow per division — annotated skeleton (whole subject, all 54 পাঠ; ✅ LOCKED) → replacement content for the flagged পাঠ (*Project 05*) → lesson plans shaped by period count (*Project 03*, D-PROJ03-018) → question banks (*Project 04*). The same workflow applies to any prepared class × subject; the Day 8–13 numbering was the pilot's relative sequence, not a limit.

### Step B — Lesson plans, পাঠ 1–16
*Work unit = **one chat per division anchor** (§4 / D-PROJ03-002), **shaped by period count (D-PROJ03-018, uniform every class × subject — supersedes the Bangla-only D-PROJ03-009):** a 1-period পাঠ = one standalone Session-Plan chat; a ≥2-period পাঠ = one Chapter-Plan chat + N Session-Plan chats (clean sessions batched ≤6 per chat under the ~20-exchange line, each flagged session its own chat when its RC lands). Each পাঠ's plan(s) are built on **REF-02 v1.6** (two-plan-types model, master D-050; §2.8 Pool retired by D-051) to the two LOCKED layout instructions — `LOCKED_ChapterPlan_Layout_Instruction_v3_2.md` + `LOCKED_SessionPlan_Layout_Instruction_v9.md` — and specialized by **REF-03 Bangla v1.1**. পাঠ 1–16 is the most-prepared slice, not a cap (D-046); the same workflow applies to any KEEP-AS-IS পাঠ in the book.*

- [ ] **B-LP.1 — Build each পাঠ in its own chat.** Buildable now: KEEP-AS-IS **পাঠ 1–8, 10, 11, 12, 15, 16**. Waiting on RC content (Project 05): **9, 13, 14** (see A→B). Per পাঠ:
  1. **Set the session count once, at chapter-planning,** from the TG Reconciliation period count — band ±40% or ±1, whichever is larger; within-band → one-line logged reason; beyond-band → Subject-Lead decision (REF-02 §2A.3). Do this before building any Session Plan.
  2. **Single-period পাঠ → one standalone Session Plan** that inlines the Layer-1 Spine. File `C1_BAN_U{nn}_SessionPlan_v1.md` (no `_S`); chat `P03 · C1 BAN · U{nn} · SessionPlan`; build to the Session-Plan layout (v8).
  3. **Multi-period পাঠ → one Chapter Plan + N Session Plans.** Chapter Plan `C1_BAN_U{nn}_ChapterPlan_v1.md` (Layer-1 Spine + §2.9 Session Map; build to the Chapter-Plan layout v3.1; chat `P03 · C1 BAN · U{nn} · ChapterPlan`). Then each period `C1_BAN_U{nn}_S{mm}_SessionPlan_v1.md` (Layers 2–3; the §3.0 header copies this session's row from the Chapter Plan's §2.9 Session Map and never re-derives the Spine; chat `P03 · C1 BAN · U{nn} · S{mm} · SessionPlan`).

## D. Session-Plan lock register — consolidated propagation 2026-08-07 (**AUTHORITATIVE**)

**Scope of this pass.** 96 `/handoffs/` files read (2026-07-10 … 2026-08-07). One (C1 BAN U23, 2026-07-19) was already applied in the v1.44/v1.45 consolidation and is re-confirmed only. **95 handoffs are newly propagated here.**

**Precedence rule (read before using §C).** Where a per-unit bullet in §C states a Session-Plan status or a Chapter-Plan version that conflicts with this register, **§D wins** — the §C bullets predate this pass and were written against superseded Chapter-Plan versions. §D also carries the Chapter-Plan version each family was actually built against. §C is not rewritten unit-by-unit in this pass (60+ in-place edits on stale anchors is a corruption risk); the conflicts are enumerated in **§D.2(e)** instead.

**What this pass did NOT do.** It minted no D-numbers, resolved no ruling, moved no file to `/archive/`, and confirmed no local `validate_plan.py` re-run. Those are Principal actions — see §D.3 and §D.4.

**Drift-register candidate (Content-Quality watch, Project-03-internal):** *Exit-Check Bank under-covers the chapter's own Bloom arc* — bank forms sit at Remember/Understand while the §2.3 arc climbs to Apply/Analyze/Create, forcing the upper Session-Map rows to mis-map. First seen in **C5 ENG U09** (caught + fixed at build; added Apply + Analyze forms); kin faults recurred at **U11** (P6 Create objective → Evaluate item) and **U12** (P7 Create capstone with no Create bank form), and then in **every one of the six C5 MATH chapter builds (2026-06-12/13)** — **U03** (P7: a named sub-skill, the ৪-rule, untested inside a covered type — type-level coverage passed while the sub-skill slipped; form ৮ extended), **U04** (S4 সমহর objective pointed at the S3 সমতুল form, zero standalone সমহর forms in the bank — items ১৭/১৮ added; plus an unsupported P12 সৃষ্টি claim withdrawn to P8), **U05** (two Session-Map objective types missing from the bank entirely — ÷১০/১০০/১০০০ and solve-type division word-problem — forcing six pointer misassignments, two onto untaught content; bank rebuilt 18→23), **U06** (কিস্তি type absent — forms ১৩/১৪ added, Row ৮ → যাচাই ১৪; যাচাই ১ rescoped forward-only to the P1 slice), **U07** (তালিকা-vs-সারণি treated as one type — bank re-typed 8→12 / 6 types, rotation re-pointed), **U08** (P16/P17 same-level *skill* mismatch + an r6 gap — forms ১৭/১৮ added, pointers re-rotated). All caught only at Principal review; the harness and a level-only by-hand check pass them. Candidate line for `DRAFT_ContentQuality_Review_Checklist_v1.md` (Chapter-Plan section): *"Does every Bloom level the §2.3 arc names have at least one matching Exit-Check form, and does each Session-Map pointer sit at its period's level?"* — see Open #5. **C5 SCI U05–U08 (2026-06-13) — first non-MATH lane:** three builds were *clean* under the guard because the bank was built type-complete from the Session Map up front — **U07** (12/6; review only refined the taxonomy, splitting the lumped ঘর্ষণ-ও-অভিকর্ষ type +2 forms), **U08** (8/4; every arc Bloom level had a matching form at build), **U06** (15/8 after A5+A8 baked) — the first non-MATH evidence that building the bank from objective *types* prevents the fault, strengthening the candidate line. **U05 added a new *dishonest-label* sub-variant:** the harness r6 PASSED on a *mislabelled* `objective_type` — a method-selection form (৮) and a particle form (৯) were filed under `classify`/`explain_reason` to pad the counts; honest re-typing exposed `classify_mixture_pure` at a single form, fixed by adding a genuine 13th classify form (bank 12→13 / 6 types). **This sharpens the tooling target:** because r6 groups by the *authored* label, even the proposed "pointer → type + named sub-skill" check misses a *mislabelled* form — the durable fix is to validate the bank's `objective_type` strings against a controlled per-subject vocabulary (or classify each form's text independently). See Open #5. **C5 MATH U09–U10 + C5 SCI U13 + the C3 BAN/MATH/SCI lanes (2026-06-14…19):** the fault class continued to split into two signatures. *Clean-at-build* (bank built type-complete from the Session Map up front): **C5 MATH U10** (14/7), **C3 BAN U16** (6/3), **C3 SCI U05** (6/3), **C3 SCI U06** (10/5, hand-checked). *Caught-at-REVIEW-or-build-fix*: **C5 MATH U09** (S1 naming-only missed the পার্থক্য half — composite form fix), **C5 SCI U13** (run/demonstrate-made-artifact type under-provisioned + যাচাই ৪ mistyped-as-build to pad r6 — a *resource-starvation* + dishonest-label combination the harness passed; fixed by adding a genuine run/output form + honest re-type + re-map), **C3 BAN U15/U18/U22/U23**, **C3 MATH U06/U07/U08/U09/U10** (U06 the largest honest rebuild 22/11; U10 a fresh dishonest-label instance — the orphaned profit form filed under `money_savings_total`), **C3 SCI U04** (the {9,10}+{11,12} dishonest pairing re-typed to honest কারণ-দাও / প্রমাণ-দাও). **Open #5 takeaway sharpened:** building the bank type-complete prevents the *coverage* miss but NOT the *authoring-time lump* (U10) — confirming the durable fix is pointer→type **plus** controlled-vocabulary `objective_type` validation (Open #5 / #5+). A separate ruling (**D-PROJ03-035**) accepted *composite single-pointer* Exit-Check forms for multi-part periods and **rejected** a multi-pointer schema extension. **The 2026-06-14 C5 batch (ENG U16–U20 · BAN U19–U23 · SCI U09–U12) adds three more signatures:** (1) *within-type sub-skill* misses on coarse `COMPREHENSION` — **C5 ENG U17** (P2 inference pointed at the P1 main-idea form; fixed at build) and **C5 ENG U19** (P3 comprehension-of-moral pointed at a SPEAKING-labelled form — **ruling owed**, Open #5+++); (2) a *missing-type* near-miss resolved correctly by **adding a genuine new type** rather than lumping — **C5 SCI U11** (`describe_impact` forms ১২/১৩, P6→১২, NOT folded under `explain_reason`); (3) further *clean-at-build* type-complete confirmations — **C5 ENG U16** (14/7), **C5 BAN U21** (12/6), **C5 SCI U09** (8/4). Two more *caught-at-review* fixes: **C5 BAN U20 v2** (P4→Analyze ৯ / P6→Create ১১, bank 8→12) and **C5 BAN U22** (+2 Analyze forms, P4 repoint ৭→১৩). Same takeaway: the durable fix is pointer→type **+** controlled-vocabulary validation; the C5 BAN U19 Option-B *composite bundle* (single two-step form for create+evaluate) is the D-PROJ03-035 pattern applied at C5 BAN.
